{% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n=== scripts/seed_demo.py\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nCreates one Admin (admin@example.test) and one Editor (editor@example.test),\nwith passwords read from CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD / CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD, never\nhard-coded. Safe to run twice. T08 adds demo content.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))\n\nfrom app import settings  # noqa: E402\nfrom app.accounts import seed_users  # noqa: E402\n\n\ndef load_dotenv(path: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Fill os.environ from .env without overriding real environment variables.\"\"\"\n    if not path.exists():\n        return\n    for line in path.read_text().splitlines():\n        line = line.strip()\n        if line and not line.startswith(\"#\") and \"=\" in line:\n            key, _, value = line.partition(\"=\")\n            os.environ.setdefault(key.strip(), value.strip())\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    load_dotenv(Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_ENV_FILE\", settings.ROOT / \".env\")))\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    database = Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_DATABASE\", settings.DATABASE_PATH))\n    seed_users(database, admin_password=admin_pw, editor_password=editor_pw)\n    print(f\"Seeded the Admin and Editor in {database}.\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n\"\"\"T01: login, logout, the two roles, and CSRF — all through the console over HTTP.\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport sqlite3\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import accounts\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom tests.conftest import DEMO_USERS, csrf_from\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nADMIN = DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"]\n\n\ndef sign_in(client, email, password):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(\"/login\"))\n    return client.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": email, \"password\": password,\n                                       \"csrf_token\": token},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_login_redirects_into_the_console(client):\n    response = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], ADMIN[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_email_is_case_insensitive(client):\n    response = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"].upper(), ADMIN[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_wrong_email_and_wrong_password_look_identical(client):\n    unknown = sign_in(client, \"nobody@example.test\", \"whatever\")\n    wrong_pw = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], \"not-the-password\")\n    assert unknown.status_code == wrong_pw.status_code == 401\n    for response in (unknown, wrong_pw):\n        assert \"wrong email or password\" in response.text.lower()\n    assert \"wrong email or password\" not in client.get(\"/login\").text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_password_never_appears_in_response_or_logs(client, caplog):\n    caplog.set_level(\"DEBUG\")\n    bad = \"hunter2-very-secret\"\n    response = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], bad)\n    assert bad not in response.text\n    assert bad not in caplog.text\n    ok = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], ADMIN[\"password\"])\n    assert ADMIN[\"password\"] not in ok.text\n    assert ADMIN[\"password\"] not in caplog.text\n\n\ndef test_passwords_are_stored_as_argon2_hashes(db_path):\n    # The one place a test reads storage: the criterion is about storage itself.\n    rows = sqlite3.connect(db_path).execute(\n        \"SELECT password_hash FROM users\").fetchall()\n---\nname: tdd\ndescription: Test-driven development. Use when the user wants to build features or fix bugs test-first, mentions \"red-green-refactor\", or wants integration tests.\n---\n\n# Test-Driven Development\n\nTDD is the red → green loop. This skill is the reference that makes that loop produce tests worth keeping: what a good test is, where tests go, the anti-patterns, and the rules of the loop. Every section applies on every cycle: consult them before and during the loop, not after.\n\nWhen exploring the codebase, read `CONTEXT.md` (if it exists) so test names and interface vocabulary match the project's domain language, and respect ADRs in the area you're touching.\n\n## What a good test is\n\nTests verify behavior through public interfaces, not implementation details. Code can change entirely; tests shouldn't. A good test reads like a specification: \"user can checkout with valid cart\" tells you exactly what capability exists, and it survives refactors because it doesn't care about internal structure.\n\nSee [tests.md](tests.md) for examples and [mocking.md](mocking.md) for mocking guidelines.\n\n## Seams: where tests go\n\nA **seam** is the public boundary you test at: the interface where you observe behavior without reaching inside. Tests live at seams, never against internals.\n\n**Test only at pre-agreed seams.** Before writing any test, write down the seams under test and confirm them with the user. No test is written at an unconfirmed seam. You can't test everything, so agreeing the seams up front is how testing effort lands on the critical paths and complex logic instead of every edge case.\n\nAsk: \"What's the public interface, and which seams should we test?\"\n\nWhen the shape of that interface is itself in question (how deep the module is, where the seam belongs, what the interface should expose), call the Skill tool with \"codebase-design\" for the vocabulary. It is the shared source of the module, interface, depth, seam, adapter, leverage and locality terms, and it is a reference to consult, not a session to run.\n\n## Anti-patterns\n\n- **Implementation-coupled**: mocks internal collaborators, tests private methods, or verifies through a side channel (querying the database instead of using the interface). The tell: the test breaks when you refactor but behavior hasn't changed.\n- **Tautological**: the assertion recomputes the expected value the way the code does (`expect(add(a, b)).toBe(a + b)`, a snapshot derived by hand the same way, a constant asserted equal to itself), so it passes by construction and can never disagree with the code. Expected values must come from an independent source of truth: a known-good literal, a worked example, the spec.\n- **Horizontal slicing**: writing all tests first, then all implementation. Bulk tests verify _imagined_ behavior: you test the _shape_ of things rather than user-facing behavior, the tests go insensitive to real changes, and you commit to test structure before understanding the implementation. Work in **vertical slices** instead: one test → one implementation → repeat, each test a **tracer bullet** that responds to what the last cycle taught you.\n\n## Rules of the loop\n\n- **Red before green.** Write the failing test first, then only enough code to pass it. Don't anticipate future tests or add speculative features.\n- **One slice at a time.** One seam, one test, one minimal implementation per cycle.\n- **Refactoring is not part of the loop.** It belongs to the review stage (see the `code-review` skill), not the red → green implementation cycle.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"8fc4e56a-9a60-4270-ab5e-9ad1beb26a1e","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:50:37.522Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"=== app/__init__.py\n\"\"\"IPHS 400 MP2 — your CMS package.\n\nT00 ships a skeleton only: one admin page, one public page, and a publish step.\nEverything else (login, roles, posts, pages, users) is yours to build, one\nticket at a time.\n\"\"\"\n=== app/accounts.py\n\"\"\"Users: argon2 hashing, authentication, and Deactivation.\n\nUsers are never deleted, only Deactivated (see CONTEXT.md).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\nfrom app import db\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n# Verified against when the email is unknown, so a miss costs as much as a hit.\n_DUMMY_HASH = _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, name: str, email: str,\n                password: str, role: str) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO users (name, email, password_hash, role, created_at) \"\n        \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (name, email.strip(), _hasher.hash(password), role,\n         datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()),\n    )\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str,\n                 password: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    \"\"\"The active User with this email and password, else None (never says why).\"\"\"\n    user = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\",\n                        (email.strip(),)).fetchone()\n    stored = user[\"password_hash\"] if user else _DUMMY_HASH\n    try:\n        _hasher.verify(stored, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return None\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef get_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef end_sessions(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Invalidate every session cookie issued to this User.\"\"\"\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET session_version = session_version + 1 \"\n                 \"WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef set_active(path: Path | str, email: str, active: bool) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Deactivate or reactivate a User. Their content and authorship stay put.\"\"\"\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET active = ? WHERE email = ?\",\n                     (int(active), email.strip()))\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef seed_users(path: Path | str, *, admin_password: str, editor_password: str,\n               admin_email: str = \"admin@example.test\",\n               editor_email: str = \"editor@example.test\") -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create the first Admin and Editor. Existing emails are left alone.\"\"\"\n    db.init_db(path)\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    try:\n        for name, email, password, role in (\n            (\"Demo Admin\", admin_email, admin_password, \"admin\"),\n            (\"Demo Editor\", editor_email, editor_password, \"editor\"),\n        ):\n            exists = conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE email = ?\",\n                                  (email,)).fetchone()\n            if not exists:\n                create_user(conn, name=name, email=email, password=password,\n                            role=role)\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n=== app/auth.py\n\"\"\"Permissions and CSRF: the checks shared by every route.\n\n- `csrf_protect` runs on every state-changing request in the app.\n- `current_user` is what makes a console route require login, and refuses a\n  Deactivated User on every request.\n- `require_admin` is the role check for Admin-only areas. Refusal is a 403.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hmac\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import accounts, db\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a request needs a signed-in User and has none.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.db_path)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    \"\"\"This session's CSRF token, created on first use.\"\"\"\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    sent = request.headers.get(\"x-csrf-token\")\n    if sent is None:\n        sent = (await request.form()).get(\"csrf_token\")\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if (not expected or not isinstance(sent, str)\n            or not hmac.compare_digest(sent, expected)):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n\n\ndef sign_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    # Fresh session and token on login, so nothing set before it carries over.\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n    request.session[\"session_version\"] = user[\"session_version\"]\n    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request,\n                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = accounts.get_user(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if (user is None or not user[\"active\"]\n            or user[\"session_version\"] != request.session.get(\"session_version\")):\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired()\n    return user\n\n\ndef require_admin(user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(current_user)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"This area is for Admins only.\")\n    return user\n=== app/cli.py\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:create_app\", factory=True, port=args.port,\n                    reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n=== app/db.py\n\"\"\"SQLite access. No ORM (ADR-002): a connection per request, plain SQL.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    name            TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    email           TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    password_hash   TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    role            TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    session_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    created_at      TEXT    NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path | str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    # Sync FastAPI dependencies and endpoints may run on different threads.\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path | str) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n=== app/main.py\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe console lives under /admin (plus /login and /logout); the public site\nanswers at /. Add routes in their own modules under app/routes/ and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import auth, db, settings\nfrom app.routes import auth as auth_routes\nfrom app.routes import console\nfrom app.web import templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database_path: Path | str | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    db_path = Path(database_path) if database_path else settings.DATABASE_PATH\n    db.init_db(db_path)\n\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\",\n                  dependencies=[Depends(auth.csrf_protect)])\n    app.state.db_path = db_path\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       max_age=8 * 60 * 60)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(auth.LoginRequired)\n    def to_login(request: Request, exc: auth.LoginRequired):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    app.include_router(auth_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(console.router)\n    return app\n=== app/publish.py\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n=== app/settings.py\n\"\"\"Configuration, read from the environment (never hard-code secrets).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nTEMPLATES = ROOT / \"templates\"\nSITE = ROOT / \"site\"\n\nSECRET_KEY = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY\", \"dev-only-not-for-production\")\nDATABASE_PATH = Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_DATABASE\", ROOT / \"cms.db\"))\nSITE_TITLE = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SITE_TITLE\", \"My CMS\")\n# Set this to your Pages URL once you deploy, e.g.\n# https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nBASE_PATH = os.environ.get(\"CMS_BASE_PATH\", \"\")\n=== app/web.py\n\"\"\"Shared Jinja environment for the console.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.auth import csrf_token\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\ntemplates.env.globals[\"csrf_token\"] = csrf_token\n=== app/routes/__init__.py\n=== app/routes/auth.py\n\"\"\"Login and logout.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import accounts, auth\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\n\nGENERIC_ERROR = \"Wrong email or password.\"\n\n\n@router.get(\"/login\")\ndef login_form(request: Request):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, \"admin/login.html\",\n                                      {\"title\": \"Sign in\", \"error\": None})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/login\")\ndef login(request: Request, email: str = Form(\"\"), password: str = Form(\"\"),\n          conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    user = accounts.authenticate(conn, email, password)\n    if user is None:\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/login.html\",\n            {\"title\": \"Sign in\", \"error\": GENERIC_ERROR}, status_code=401)\n    auth.sign_in(request, user)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/logout\")\ndef logout(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n           conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    accounts.end_sessions(conn, user[\"id\"])\n    request.session.clear()\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n=== app/routes/console.py\n\"\"\"The console: every route here requires a signed-in User.\n\nThe router-level dependency is what guarantees that, so a new route can't\nforget it. Pages and Users are Admin-only placeholders until T06 and T07.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import auth\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.current_user)])\n\n\ndef page(request: Request, template: str, user: sqlite3.Row, title: str):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, template,\n                                      {\"title\": title, \"user\": user})\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef dashboard(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/dashboard.html\", user, \"Dashboard\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\ndef pages(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/placeholder.html\", user, \"Pages\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/users\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\ndef users(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/placeholder.html\", user, \"Users\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{path:path}\")\ndef not_found(path: str):\n    # Signed-out visitors were already redirected; this is a real 404.\n    raise HTTPException(404, \"Not found\")\n=== tests/conftest.py\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\nEach test gets its own temporary database, seeded with the demo Admin and\nEditor, so tests never share state.\n\n`client` gives you an anonymous client. `client_as(role)` gives you a client\nthat is signed in as a seeded user of that role, so access-control tests stay\none line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code == 403\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.accounts import seed_users\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\nCSRF_FIELD = re.compile(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"')\n\n\ndef csrf_from(response) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Pull the CSRF token out of a rendered form.\"\"\"\n    match = CSRF_FIELD.search(response.text)\n    assert match, \"no csrf_token field in the page\"\n    return match.group(1)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef db_path(tmp_path):\n    path = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n    seed_users(path, admin_password=DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"],\n               editor_password=DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"password\"])\n    return path\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(db_path) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app(db_path))\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(db_path):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a signed-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app(db_path))\n        token = csrf_from(c.get(\"/login\"))\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n                                          \"csrf_token\": token},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        assert response.status_code in (302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n=== tests/test_t00.py\n\"\"\"T00: the walking skeleton. These pass in the fresh template.\n\nDo not delete them. If a change breaks them, the change broke the app's front\ndoor.\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef test_admin_console_answers(client_as):\n    # T01 put the console behind login, so the front door is now \"signed in\".\n    response = client_as(\"admin\").get(\"/admin\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    assert \"dashboard\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_public_home_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_publish_writes_a_site(tmp_path):\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    assert (out / \"index.html\").exists() and (out / \"style.css\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_published_html_uses_relative_paths(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Root-absolute paths break on GitHub Pages project URLs (rubric F2).\"\"\"\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    html = (render_site(tmp_path / \"site\") / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"/' not in html and 'src=\"/' not in html\n=== templates/base.html\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a></header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n=== templates/admin/dashboard.html\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block console %}\n  <h1>Dashboard</h1>\n  <p>Signed in as {{ user.name }} ({{ user.role }}).</p>\n{% endblock %}\n=== templates/admin/layout.html\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <nav>\n    <a href=\"/admin\">Dashboard</a>\n    {% if user.role == \"admin\" %}\n      <a href=\"/admin/pages\">Pages</a>\n      <a href=\"/admin/users\">Users</a>\n    {% endif %}\n    <form method=\"post\" action=\"/logout\" style=\"display:inline\">\n      <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n      <button type=\"submit\">Sign out</button>\n    </form>\n  </nav>\n  {% block console %}{% endblock %}\n{% endblock %}\n=== templates/admin/login.html\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Sign in</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/login\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n    <p><label>Email <input type=\"email\" name=\"email\" required autofocus></label></p>\n    <p><label>Password <input type=\"password\" name=\"password\" required></label></p>\n    <button type=\"submit\">Sign in</button>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n=== templates/admin/placeholder.html\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block console %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  <p>Coming soon.</p>\n{% endblock %}\n=== templates/public/home.html\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  {% if items %}\n    <ul>{% for item in items %}<li><a href=\"{{ item.href }}\">{{ item.title }}</a></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n  {% else %}\n    <p>Nothing published yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n=== scripts/seed_demo.py\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nCreates one Admin (admin@example.test) and one Editor (editor@example.test),\nwith passwords read from CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD / CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD, never\nhard-coded. Safe to run twice. T08 adds demo content.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))\n\nfrom app import settings  # noqa: E402\nfrom app.accounts import seed_users  # noqa: E402\n\n\ndef load_dotenv(path: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Fill os.environ from .env without overriding real environment variables.\"\"\"\n    if not path.exists():\n        return\n    for line in path.read_text().splitlines():\n        line = line.strip()\n        if line and not line.startswith(\"#\") and \"=\" in line:\n            key, _, value = line.partition(\"=\")\n            os.environ.setdefault(key.strip(), value.strip())\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    load_dotenv(Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_ENV_FILE\", settings.ROOT / \".env\")))\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    database = Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_DATABASE\", settings.DATABASE_PATH))\n    seed_users(database, admin_password=admin_pw, editor_password=editor_pw)\n    print(f\"Seeded the Admin and Editor in {database}.\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n\"\"\"T01: login, logout, the two roles, and CSRF — all through the console over HTTP.\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport sqlite3\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import accounts\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom tests.conftest import DEMO_USERS, csrf_from\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nADMIN = DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"]\n\n\ndef sign_in(client, email, password):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(\"/login\"))\n    return client.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": email, \"password\": password,\n                                       \"csrf_token\": token},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_login_redirects_into_the_console(client):\n    response = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], ADMIN[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_email_is_case_insensitive(client):\n    response = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"].upper(), ADMIN[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_wrong_email_and_wrong_password_look_identical(client):\n    unknown = sign_in(client, \"nobody@example.test\", \"whatever\")\n    wrong_pw = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], \"not-the-password\")\n    assert unknown.status_code == wrong_pw.status_code == 401\n    for response in (unknown, wrong_pw):\n        assert \"wrong email or password\" in response.text.lower()\n    assert \"wrong email or password\" not in client.get(\"/login\").text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_password_never_appears_in_response_or_logs(client, caplog):\n    caplog.set_level(\"DEBUG\")\n    bad = \"hunter2-very-secret\"\n    response = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], bad)\n    assert bad not in response.text\n    assert bad not in caplog.text\n    ok = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], ADMIN[\"password\"])\n    assert ADMIN[\"password\"] not in ok.text\n    assert ADMIN[\"password\"] not in caplog.text\n\n\ndef test_passwords_are_stored_as_argon2_hashes(db_path):\n    # The one place a test reads storage: the criterion is about storage itself.\n    rows = sqlite3.connect(db_path).execute(\n        \"SELECT password_hash FROM users\").fetchall()\n---\nname: tdd\ndescription: Test-driven development. Use when the user wants to build features or fix bugs test-first, mentions \"red-green-refactor\", or wants integration tests.\n---\n\n# Test-Driven Development\n\nTDD is the red → green loop. This skill is the reference that makes that loop produce tests worth keeping: what a good test is, where tests go, the anti-patterns, and the rules of the loop. Every section applies on every cycle: consult them before and during the loop, not after.\n\nWhen exploring the codebase, read `CONTEXT.md` (if it exists) so test names and interface vocabulary match the project's domain language, and respect ADRs in the area you're touching.\n\n## What a good test is\n\nTests verify behavior through public interfaces, not implementation details. Code can change entirely; tests shouldn't. A good test reads like a specification: \"user can checkout with valid cart\" tells you exactly what capability exists, and it survives refactors because it doesn't care about internal structure.\n\nSee [tests.md](tests.md) for examples and [mocking.md](mocking.md) for mocking guidelines.\n\n## Seams: where tests go\n\nA **seam** is the public boundary you test at: the interface where you observe behavior without reaching inside. Tests live at seams, never against internals.\n\n**Test only at pre-agreed seams.** Before writing any test, write down the seams under test and confirm them with the user. No test is written at an unconfirmed seam. You can't test everything, so agreeing the seams up front is how testing effort lands on the critical paths and complex logic instead of every edge case.\n\nAsk: \"What's the public interface, and which seams should we test?\"\n\nWhen the shape of that interface is itself in question (how deep the module is, where the seam belongs, what the interface should expose), call the Skill tool with \"codebase-design\" for the vocabulary. It is the shared source of the module, interface, depth, seam, adapter, leverage and locality terms, and it is a reference to consult, not a session to run.\n\n## Anti-patterns\n\n- **Implementation-coupled**: mocks internal collaborators, tests private methods, or verifies through a side channel (querying the database instead of using the interface). The tell: the test breaks when you refactor but behavior hasn't changed.\n- **Tautological**: the assertion recomputes the expected value the way the code does (`expect(add(a, b)).toBe(a + b)`, a snapshot derived by hand the same way, a constant asserted equal to itself), so it passes by construction and can never disagree with the code. Expected values must come from an independent source of truth: a known-good literal, a worked example, the spec.\n- **Horizontal slicing**: writing all tests first, then all implementation. Bulk tests verify _imagined_ behavior: you test the _shape_ of things rather than user-facing behavior, the tests go insensitive to real changes, and you commit to test structure before understanding the implementation. Work in **vertical slices** instead: one test → one implementation → repeat, each test a **tracer bullet** that responds to what the last cycle taught you.\n\n## Rules of the loop\n\n- **Red before green.** Write the failing test first, then only enough code to pass it. Don't anticipate future tests or add speculative features.\n- **One slice at a time.** One seam, one test, one minimal implementation per cycle.\n- **Refactoring is not part of the loop.** It belongs to the review stage (see the `code-review` skill), not the red → green implementation cycle.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"163c832b-8a26-4662-8275-811ed63511a0","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":3},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"76150151-abb2-49a8-bb90-5c408242c68f","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"2f6a98a8-13ff-4356-8cfb-a9aeae7fc191","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"ea1aabcb-2929-48ab-a631-677fc88cf8b1","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfYK79UyqS1bbzDTGJqrJ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NJ6NAAGJYspiGo6ru54veG","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t02_posts.py","content":"\"\"\"T02: create and edit a Draft Post, through the console over HTTP (Seam 1).\"\"\"\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom zoneinfo import ZoneInfo\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from\n\n\ndef save_post(client, *, title=\"Menú de la semana\", kind=\"menu\", body=\"Hola\",\n              slug=\"\"):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n    return client.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\n        \"csrf_token\": token, \"kind\": kind, \"title\": title, \"slug\": slug,\n        \"body_md\": body}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef edit_post(client, location, **fields):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(location))\n    return client.post(location, data={\"csrf_token\": token, **fields},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_editor_creates_a_draft_of_each_kind_with_themselves_as_author(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    for kind in (\"news\", \"event\", \"menu\"):\n        response = save_post(editor, title=f\"Un {kind}\", kind=kind)\n        assert response.status_code == 303\n    page = editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    for kind in (\"News\", \"Event\", \"Menu\"):\n        assert kind in page\n    assert page.count(\"Draft\") >= 3\n    assert page.count(\"Demo Editor\") >= 3\n\n\ndef test_post_forms_carry_the_csrf_token(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = save_post(editor).headers[\"location\"]\n    assert 'name=\"csrf_token\"' in editor.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").text\n    assert 'name=\"csrf_token\"' in editor.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_post_form_without_csrf_token_is_refused(client_as):\n    response = client_as(\"editor\").post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\n        \"kind\": \"news\", \"title\": \"x\", \"body_md\": \"y\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_signed_out_visitor_is_sent_to_login(client):\n    assert client.get(\"/admin/posts\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_kind_cannot_be_changed_after_creation(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = save_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Solo noticia\").headers[\"location\"]\n    edit_post(editor, location, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Solo noticia\",\n              slug=\"solo-noticia\", body_md=\"cambio\")\n    page = editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"News\" in page and \"Menu\" not in page\n    assert 'name=\"kind\"' not in editor.get(location).text\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"title, expected\", [\n    (\"Menú de la semana\", \"menu-de-la-semana\"),\n    (\"Cóctel de Año Nuevo: ¡Ñandú!\", \"coctel-de-ano-nuevo-nandu\"),\n])\ndef test_slug_is_generated_from_the_title(client_as, title, expected):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = save_post(editor, title=title).headers[\"location\"]\n    assert f'value=\"{expected}\"' in editor.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_auto_generated_slug_clash_gets_a_numeric_suffix(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    slugs = []\n    for _ in range(3):\n        location = save_post(editor).headers[\"location\"]\n        slugs.append(location)\n    assert 'value=\"menu-de-la-semana\"' in editor.get(slugs[0]).text\n    assert 'value=\"menu-de-la-semana-2\"' in editor.get(slugs[1]).text\n    assert 'value=\"menu-de-la-semana-3\"' in editor.get(slugs[2]).text\n\n\ndef test_same_slug_in_another_kind_is_not_a_clash(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    save_post(editor, title=\"Torneo\", kind=\"news\")\n    location = save_post(editor, title=\"Torneo\", kind=\"event\").headers[\"location\"]\n    assert 'value=\"torneo\"' in editor.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_hand_typed_slug_clash_is_a_form_error_and_saves_nothing(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    save_post(editor, title=\"Primero\", slug=\"mi-slug\")\n    response = save_post(editor, title=\"Segundo\", slug=\"mi-slug\")\n    assert response.status_code == 422\n    assert \"already\" in response.text.lower()\n    assert \"Segundo\" in response.text  # the form keeps what was typed\n    assert \"Segundo\" not in editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n\n\ndef test_editing_to_a_taken_slug_is_a_form_error(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    save_post(editor, title=\"Primero\", slug=\"primero\")\n    location = save_post(editor, title=\"Segundo\", slug=\"segundo\").headers[\"location\"]\n    response = edit_post(editor, location, title=\"Segundo\", slug=\"primero\",\n                         body_md=\"x\")\n    assert response.status_code == 422\n    assert 'value=\"segundo\"' in editor.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_slug_can_be_edited_and_is_kept_when_the_title_changes(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = save_post(editor, title=\"Un título muy largo\").headers[\"location\"]\n    edit_post(editor, location, title=\"Un título muy largo\", slug=\"corto\",\n              body_md=\"x\")\n    edit_post(editor, location, title=\"Otro título\", slug=\"corto\", body_md=\"x\")\n    page = editor.get(location).text\n    assert 'value=\"corto\"' in page and \"Otro título\" in page\n\n\ndef test_another_editor_can_edit_and_the_author_stays(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = save_post(editor, title=\"De la editora\").headers[\"location\"]\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    response = edit_post(admin, location, title=\"Corregido por admin\",\n                         slug=\"de-la-editora\", body_md=\"nuevo\")\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    row = next(line for line in admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text.splitlines()\n               if \"Corregido por admin\" in line or \"Demo\" in line)\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Corregido por admin\" in page\n    assert \"Demo Editor\" in page and \"Demo Admin\" not in page.split(\"<table\")[1]\n    assert row\n\n\ndef test_preview_sanitizes_markdown_and_keeps_external_images(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    body = (\"Hola\\n\\n<script>alert('x')</script>\\n\\n\"\n            \"![foto](https://example.com/foto.png)\")\n    location = save_post(editor, body=body).headers[\"location\"]\n    html = editor.get(f\"{location}/preview\").text\n    assert \"<script\" not in html\n    assert '<img src=\"https://example.com/foto.png\"' in html\n\n\ndef test_preview_uses_the_spanish_layout_and_shows_no_author(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = save_post(editor, title=\"Cena de gala\", kind=\"event\").headers[\"location\"]\n    html = editor.get(f\"{location}/preview\").text\n    assert '<html lang=\"es\">' in html\n    assert \"Cena de gala\" in html\n    assert \"Evento\" in html\n    assert \"Demo Editor\" not in html\n\n\ndef test_post_list_shows_last_updated_in_lima_time(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    lima = ZoneInfo(\"America/Lima\")\n    before = datetime.now(lima).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n    save_post(editor)\n    after = datetime.now(lima).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n    page = editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert before in page or after in page\n\n\ndef test_post_list_shows_every_post_to_every_editor(client_as):\n    client_as(\"admin\")  # separate session, same database\n    save_post(client_as(\"admin\"), title=\"Del admin\")\n    save_post(client_as(\"editor\"), title=\"De la editora\")\n    page = client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Del admin\" in page and \"De la editora\" in page\n\n\ndef test_missing_title_and_unknown_kind_are_rejected(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert save_post(editor, title=\"   \").status_code == 422\n    assert save_post(editor, kind=\"poem\").status_code == 422\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3404,"cache_read_input_tokens":61952,"output_tokens":3043,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3404,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3043,"cache_read_input_tokens":61952,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3404,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3404},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01NJ6NAAGJYspiGo6ru54veG":{"file_path":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t02_posts.py","content":"\"\"\"T02: create and edit a Draft Post, through the console over HTTP (Seam 1).\"\"\"\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom zoneinfo import ZoneInfo\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_from\n\n\ndef save_post(client, *, title=\"Menú de la semana\", kind=\"menu\", body=\"Hola\",\n              slug=\"\"):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(\"/admin/posts/new\"))\n    return client.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\n        \"csrf_token\": token, \"kind\": kind, \"title\": title, \"slug\": slug,\n        \"body_md\": body}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef edit_post(client, location, **fields):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(location))\n    return client.post(location, data={\"csrf_token\": token, **fields},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_editor_creates_a_draft_of_each_kind_with_themselves_as_author(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    for kind in (\"news\", \"event\", \"menu\"):\n        response = save_post(editor, title=f\"Un {kind}\", kind=kind)\n        assert response.status_code == 303\n    page = editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    for kind in (\"News\", \"Event\", \"Menu\"):\n        assert kind in page\n    assert page.count(\"Draft\") >= 3\n    assert page.count(\"Demo Editor\") >= 3\n\n\ndef test_post_forms_carry_the_csrf_token(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = save_post(editor).headers[\"location\"]\n    assert 'name=\"csrf_token\"' in editor.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").text\n    assert 'name=\"csrf_token\"' in editor.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_post_form_without_csrf_token_is_refused(client_as):\n    response = client_as(\"editor\").post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\n        \"kind\": \"news\", \"title\": \"x\", \"body_md\": \"y\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_signed_out_visitor_is_sent_to_login(client):\n    assert client.get(\"/admin/posts\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_kind_cannot_be_changed_after_creation(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = save_post(editor, kind=\"news\", title=\"Solo noticia\").headers[\"location\"]\n    edit_post(editor, location, kind=\"menu\", title=\"Solo noticia\",\n              slug=\"solo-noticia\", body_md=\"cambio\")\n    page = editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"News\" in page and \"Menu\" not in page\n    assert 'name=\"kind\"' not in editor.get(location).text\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"title, expected\", [\n    (\"Menú de la semana\", \"menu-de-la-semana\"),\n    (\"Cóctel de Año Nuevo: ¡Ñandú!\", \"coctel-de-ano-nuevo-nandu\"),\n])\ndef test_slug_is_generated_from_the_title(client_as, title, expected):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = save_post(editor, title=title).headers[\"location\"]\n    assert f'value=\"{expected}\"' in editor.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_auto_generated_slug_clash_gets_a_numeric_suffix(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    slugs = []\n    for _ in range(3):\n        location = save_post(editor).headers[\"location\"]\n        slugs.append(location)\n    assert 'value=\"menu-de-la-semana\"' in editor.get(slugs[0]).text\n    assert 'value=\"menu-de-la-semana-2\"' in editor.get(slugs[1]).text\n    assert 'value=\"menu-de-la-semana-3\"' in editor.get(slugs[2]).text\n\n\ndef test_same_slug_in_another_kind_is_not_a_clash(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    save_post(editor, title=\"Torneo\", kind=\"news\")\n    location = save_post(editor, title=\"Torneo\", kind=\"event\").headers[\"location\"]\n    assert 'value=\"torneo\"' in editor.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_hand_typed_slug_clash_is_a_form_error_and_saves_nothing(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    save_post(editor, title=\"Primero\", slug=\"mi-slug\")\n    response = save_post(editor, title=\"Segundo\", slug=\"mi-slug\")\n    assert response.status_code == 422\n    assert \"already\" in response.text.lower()\n    assert \"Segundo\" in response.text  # the form keeps what was typed\n    assert \"Segundo\" not in editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n\n\ndef test_editing_to_a_taken_slug_is_a_form_error(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    save_post(editor, title=\"Primero\", slug=\"primero\")\n    location = save_post(editor, title=\"Segundo\", slug=\"segundo\").headers[\"location\"]\n    response = edit_post(editor, location, title=\"Segundo\", slug=\"primero\",\n                         body_md=\"x\")\n    assert response.status_code == 422\n    assert 'value=\"segundo\"' in editor.get(location).text\n\n\ndef test_slug_can_be_edited_and_is_kept_when_the_title_changes(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = save_post(editor, title=\"Un título muy largo\").headers[\"location\"]\n    edit_post(editor, location, title=\"Un título muy largo\", slug=\"corto\",\n              body_md=\"x\")\n    edit_post(editor, location, title=\"Otro título\", slug=\"corto\", body_md=\"x\")\n    page = editor.get(location).text\n    assert 'value=\"corto\"' in page and \"Otro título\" in page\n\n\ndef test_another_editor_can_edit_and_the_author_stays(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    location = save_post(editor, title=\"De la editora\").headers[\"location\"]\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    response = edit_post(admin, location, title=\"Corregido por admin\",\n                         slug=\"de-la-editora\", body_md=\"nuevo\")\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    row = next(line for line in admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text.splitlines()\n               if \"Corregido por admin\" in line or \"Demo\" in line)\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Corregido por admin\" in page\n    assert \"Demo Editor\" in page and \"Demo Admin\" not in page.split(\"<table\")[1]\n    assert row\n\n\ndef test_preview_sanitizes_markdown_and_keeps_external_images(client_as):\n Used by Preview and, later, Export.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport nh3\nfrom markdown_it import MarkdownIt\n\n_md = MarkdownIt(\"commonmark\")\n\n\ndef render_markdown(text: str) -> str:\n    # http(s)/mailto only, and no relative URLs: an image must be hosted elsewhere.\n    return nh3.clean(_md.render(text), url_schemes={\"http\", \"https\", \"mailto\"},\n                     url_relative=\"deny\")\nEOF\ncat > app/content.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Posts: creation, editing, Slugs and the list. Shared by the console and Export.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nimport unicodedata\n\nfrom app.timeutil import utc_now\n\nKINDS = (\"news\", \"event\", \"menu\")\n\n\nclass PostError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A problem with what the User typed, worded for the form.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef slugify(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Lower-case ASCII, accents stripped, hyphens between words.\"\"\"\n    plain = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", text).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", plain.lower()).strip(\"-\")\n\n\ndef _taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str, slug: str,\n           except_id: int | None) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE kind = ? AND slug = ? AND id IS NOT ?\",\n                        (kind, slug, except_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef _slug_for(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str, title: str, typed: str,\n              except_id: int | None = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A typed Slug must be free; a generated one gets -2, -3, ... until it is.\"\"\"\n    if typed.strip():\n        slug = slugify(typed)\n        if not slug:\n            raise PostError(\"The slug needs letters or numbers.\")\n        if _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n            raise PostError(f\"The slug “{slug}” is already used by another post of this kind.\")\n        return slug\n    base = slugify(title)\n    if not base:\n        raise PostError(\"The title needs letters or numbers to make a slug from.\")\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef _clean_title(title: str) -> str:\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise PostError(\"The title is required.\")\n    return title\n\n\ndef create_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, kind: str, title: str, slug: str,\n                body_md: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    if kind not in KINDS:\n        raise PostError(\"Choose News, Event or Menu.\")\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    slug = _slug_for(conn, kind, title, slug)\n    now = utc_now()\n    cur = conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO posts (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, created_at, \"\n        \"updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, now, now))\n    conn.commit()\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str,\n                slug: str, body_md: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Kind and Author never change. The Slug is locked once first published.\"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    if post[\"first_published_at\"]:\n        slug = post[\"slug\"]\n    else:\n        slug = _slug_for(conn, post[\"kind\"], title, slug or post[\"slug\"], post[\"id\"])\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, slug = ?, body_md = ?, updated_at = ? \"\n                 \"WHERE id = ?\", (title, slug, body_md, utc_now(), post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef get_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT posts.*, users.name AS author_name FROM posts \"\n        \"JOIN users ON users.id = posts.author_id \"\n        \"ORDER BY posts.updated_at DESC, posts.id DESC\").fetchall()\nEOF\ncat > app/routes/posts.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Post list, editor and Preview. Editors and Admins can do all of it.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, content\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/posts\")\n\nKIND_LABELS = {\"news\": \"News\", \"event\": \"Event\", \"menu\": \"Menu\"}\nKIND_LABELS_ES = {\"news\": \"Noticia\", \"event\": \"Evento\", \"menu\": \"Menú de la semana\"}\n\n\ndef form_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, post: dict, *,\n              error: str | None = None, status_code: int = 200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_form.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit post\" if post.get(\"id\") else \"New post\", \"user\": user,\n         \"post\": post, \"error\": error, \"kinds\": KIND_LABELS,\n         \"kind_labels\": KIND_LABELS},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\ndef load(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    post = content.get_post(conn, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"Post not found\")\n    return post\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef post_list(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"posts\": content.list_posts(conn),\n         \"kind_labels\": KIND_LABELS})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_post(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return form_page(request, user, {\"kind\": \"news\", \"title\": \"\", \"slug\": \"\",\n                                     \"body_md\": \"\"})\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef create_post(request: Request, kind: str = Form(\"\"), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n                slug: str = Form(\"\"), body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n                user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    try:\n        post_id = content.create_post(conn, kind=kind, title=title, slug=slug,\n                                      body_md=body_md, author_id=user[\"id\"])\n    except content.PostError as exc:\n        return form_page(request, user, {\"kind\": kind, \"title\": title,\n                                         \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md},\n                         error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef edit_post(request: Request, post_id: int,\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return form_page(request, user, dict(load(conn, post_id)))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef save_post(request: Request, post_id: int, title: str = Form(\"\"),\n              slug: str = Form(\"\"), body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    try:\n        content.update_post(conn, post, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md)\n    except content.PostError as exc:\n        return form_page(request, user, {**dict(post), \"title\": title,\n                                         \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md},\n                         error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/preview\")\ndef preview(request: Request, post_id: int,\n            conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"public/post.html\",\n        {\"title\": post[\"title\"], \"lang\": \"es\", \"css_path\": \"/style.css\",\n         \"home_path\": \"/\", \"post\": post,\n         \"kind_label\": KIND_LABELS_ES[post[\"kind\"]],\n         \"body_html\": render_markdown(post[\"body_md\"])})\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/post_list.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block console %}\n  <h1>Posts</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New post</a></p>\n  {% if posts %}\n  <table>\n    <thead><tr><th>Title</th><th>Kind</th><th>Status</th><th>Author</th><th>Last updated (Lima)</th></tr></thead>\n    <tbody>\n    {% for p in posts %}\n      <tr>\n        <td><a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ p.id }}\">{{ p.title }}</a></td>\n        <td>{{ kind_labels[p.kind] }}</td>\n        <td>{{ p.status | capitalize }}</td>\n        <td>{{ p.author_name }}</td>\n        <td>{{ p.updated_at | lima }}</td>\n      </tr>\n    {% endfor %}\n    </tbody>\n  </table>\n  {% else %}\n    <p>No posts yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/post_form.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block console %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"{% if post.id %}/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}{% else %}/admin/posts{% endif %}\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n    {% if post.id %}\n      <p>Kind: {{ kind_labels[post.kind] }} · Status: {{ post.status | capitalize }}</p>\n    {% else %}\n      <p><label>Kind\n        <select name=\"kind\">\n          {% for value, label in kinds.items() %}\n            <option value=\"{{ value }}\"{% if post.kind == value %} selected{% endif %}>{{ label }}</option>\n          {% endfor %}\n        </select></label></p>\n    {% endif %}\n    <p><label>Title <input type=\"text\" name=\"title\" value=\"{{ post.title }}\" required></label></p>\n    <p><label>Slug <input type=\"text\" name=\"slug\" value=\"{{ post.slug }}\"\n      {% if post.first_published_at %}readonly{% endif AND slug = ? AND id IS NOT ?\",\n                        (kind, slug, except_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef _slug_for(conn: sqlite3.Connection, kind: str, title: str, typed: str,\n              except_id: int | None = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"A typed Slug must be free; a generated one gets -2, -3, ... until it is.\"\"\"\n    if typed.strip():\n        slug = slugify(typed)\n        if not slug:\n            raise PostError(\"The slug needs letters or numbers.\")\n        if _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n            raise PostError(f\"The slug “{slug}” is already used by another post of this kind.\")\n        return slug\n    base = slugify(title)\n    if not base:\n        raise PostError(\"The title needs letters or numbers to make a slug from.\")\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while _taken(conn, kind, slug, except_id):\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef _clean_title(title: str) -> str:\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise PostError(\"The title is required.\")\n    return title\n\n\ndef create_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, kind: str, title: str, slug: str,\n                body_md: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    if kind not in KINDS:\n        raise PostError(\"Choose News, Event or Menu.\")\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    slug = _slug_for(conn, kind, title, slug)\n    now = utc_now()\n    cur = conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO posts (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, created_at, \"\n        \"updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (kind, title, slug, body_md, author_id, now, now))\n    conn.commit()\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post: sqlite3.Row, *, title: str,\n                slug: str, body_md: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Kind and Author never change. The Slug is locked once first published.\"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    if post[\"first_published_at\"]:\n        slug = post[\"slug\"]\n    else:\n        slug = _slug_for(conn, post[\"kind\"], title, slug or post[\"slug\"], post[\"id\"])\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, slug = ?, body_md = ?, updated_at = ? \"\n                 \"WHERE id = ?\", (title, slug, body_md, utc_now(), post[\"id\"]))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef get_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    return conn.execute(\n        \"SELECT posts.*, users.name AS author_name FROM posts \"\n        \"JOIN users ON users.id = posts.author_id \"\n        \"ORDER BY posts.updated_at DESC, posts.id DESC\").fetchall()\nEOF\ncat > app/routes/posts.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Post list, editor and Preview. Editors and Admins can do all of it.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, content\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/posts\")\n\nKIND_LABELS = {\"news\": \"News\", \"event\": \"Event\", \"menu\": \"Menu\"}\nKIND_LABELS_ES = {\"news\": \"Noticia\", \"event\": \"Evento\", \"menu\": \"Menú de la semana\"}\n\n\ndef form_page(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row, post: dict, *,\n              error: str | None = None, status_code: int = 200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_form.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit post\" if post.get(\"id\") else \"New post\", \"user\": user,\n         \"post\": post, \"error\": error, \"kinds\": KIND_LABELS,\n         \"kind_labels\": KIND_LABELS},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\ndef load(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    post = content.get_post(conn, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"Post not found\")\n    return post\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef post_list(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"posts\": content.list_posts(conn),\n         \"kind_labels\": KIND_LABELS})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_post(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return form_page(request, user, {\"kind\": \"news\", \"title\": \"\", \"slug\": \"\",\n                                     \"body_md\": \"\"})\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef create_post(request: Request, kind: str = Form(\"\"), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n                slug: str = Form(\"\"), body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n                user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n                conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    try:\n        post_id = content.create_post(conn, kind=kind, title=title, slug=slug,\n                                      body_md=body_md, author_id=user[\"id\"])\n    except content.PostError as exc:\n        return form_page(request, user, {\"kind\": kind, \"title\": title,\n                                         \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md},\n                         error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef edit_post(request: Request, post_id: int,\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    return form_page(request, user, dict(load(conn, post_id)))\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef save_post(request: Request, post_id: int, title: str = Form(\"\"),\n              slug: str = Form(\"\"), body_md: str = Form(\"\"),\n              user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n              conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    try:\n        content.update_post(conn, post, title=title, slug=slug, body_md=body_md)\n    except content.PostError as exc:\n        return form_page(request, user, {**dict(post), \"title\": title,\n                                         \"slug\": slug, \"body_md\": body_md},\n                         error=str(exc), status_code=422)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/preview\")\ndef preview(request: Request, post_id: int,\n            conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    post = load(conn, post_id)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"public/post.html\",\n        {\"title\": post[\"title\"], \"lang\": \"es\", \"css_path\": \"/style.css\",\n         \"home_path\": \"/\", \"post\": post,\n         \"kind_label\": KIND_LABELS_ES[post[\"kind\"]],\n         \"body_html\": render_markdown(post[\"body_md\"])})\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/post_list.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block console %}\n  <h1>Posts</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New post</a></p>\n  {% if posts %}\n  <table>\n    <thead><tr><th>Title</th><th>Kind</th><th>Status</th><th>Author</th><th>Last updated (Lima)</th></tr></thead>\n    <tbody>\n    {% for p in posts %}\n      <tr>\n        <td><a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ p.id }}\">{{ p.title }}</a></td>\n        <td>{{ kind_labels[p.kind] }}</td>\n        <td>{{ p.status | capitalize }}</td>\n        <td>{{ p.author_name }}</td>\n        <td>{{ p.updated_at | lima }}</td>\n      </tr>\n    {% endfor %}\n    </tbody>\n  </table>\n  {% else %}\n    <p>No posts yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/post_form.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"admin/layout.html\" %}\n{% block console %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"{% if post.id %}/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}{% else %}/admin/posts{% endif %}\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token(request) }}\">\n    {% if post.id %}\n      <p>Kind: {{ kind_labels[post.kind] }} · Status: {{ post.status | capitalize }}</p>\n    {% else %}\n      <p><label>Kind\n        <select name=\"kind\">\n          {% for value, label in kinds.items() %}\n            <option value=\"{{ value }}\"{% if post.kind == value %} selected{% endif %}>{{ label }}</option>\n          {% endfor %}\n        </select></label></p>\n    {% endif %}\n    <p><label>Title <input type=\"text\" name=\"title\" value=\"{{ post.title }}\" required></label></p>\n    <p><label>Slug <input type=\"text\" name=\"slug\" value=\"{{ post.slug }}\"\n      {% if post.first_published_at %}readonly{% endif %}></label>\n      {% if not post.id %}<small>Leave blank to generate it from the title.</small>{% endif %}</p>\n    <p><label>Body (Markdown)<br>\n      <textarea name=\"body_md\" rows=\"14\" cols=\"70\">{{ post.body_md }}</textarea></label></p>\n    <button type=\"submit\">Save draft</button>\n    {% if post.id %}<a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/preview\" target=\"_blank\">Preview</a>{% endif %}\n    <a href=\"/admin/posts\">Back to posts</a>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\ncat > templates/public/post.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% Pin the fixed point\n\nWhatever the user said is the fixed point (a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc.). If they didn't specify one, ask for it.\n\nCapture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.\n\nBefore going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here, not inside two parallel sub-agents.\n\n### 2. Identify the spec source\n\nLook for the originating spec, in this order:\n\n1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.), fetched via the workflow in `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\n2. A path the user passed as an argument.\n3. A spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.\n4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report \"no spec available\".\n\n### 3. Identify the standards sources\n\nAnything in the repo that documents how code should be written, such as `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md`.\n\nOn top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below: a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:\n\n- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.\n- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic (\"possible Feature Envy\"), never a hard violation. Like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.\n\nEach smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:\n\n- **Mysterious Name**: a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.\n- **Duplicated Code**: the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.\n- **Feature Envy**: a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.\n- **Data Clumps**: the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.\n- **Primitive Obsession**: a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.\n- **Repeated Switches**: the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.\n- **Shotgun Surgery**: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.\n- **Divergent Change**: one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.\n- **Speculative Generality**: abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.\n- **Message Chains**: long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.\n- **Middle Man**: a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.\n- **Refused Bequest**: a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.\n\n### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel\n\n**Standards sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The full diff command and commit list.\n- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full (the sub-agent has no other access to it).\n- The brief: \"Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.\"\n\n**Spec sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The diff command and commit list.\n- The path or fetched contents of the spec.\n- The brief: \"Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words.\"\n\nIf the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.\n\n### 5. Aggregate\n\nPresent the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings, because the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).\n\nEnd with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes: that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.\n\n## Why two axes\n\nA change can pass one axis and fail the other:\n\n- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**\n- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**\n\nReporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: since HEAD (uncommitted T02 work), spec issue #1, ticket #3"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"a93ce8d0-9c01-431d-b5d5-beee21ba3639","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:52:46.885Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_01RbHRoDMMFABhnf5bobTFVD","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"2f6a98a8-13ff-4356-8cfb-a9aeae7fc191","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
